- **anf-feature-cycle**: ANF 클론에 ANF 기능 하나를 추가하는 전체 사이클을 조율한다. 사이클은 조사, 설계 상의, 구현, WSL 실환경 검증, 개념 문서 순서다. "스냅샷 기능 만들어줘", "용량 풀 구현하자", "다음 기능 진행", "export policy 추가"처럼 ANF 기능을 구현·추가·확장하자는 요청에 사용한다. "스냅샷 재검증해줘", "볼륨 개념 문서만 다시 써줘", "조사부터 다시", "이전 결과 바탕으로 보완", "결과 개선" 같은 후속 요청에도 사용한다. 남은 기능 여러 개를 이어서 끝까지 맡기는 요청("남은 기능 끝까지 진행해", "로드맵 진행해")에는 anf-roadmap을 사용한다. 기능과 무관한 단순 질문, 버그 하나 수정, 환경 설정 질문에는 사용하지 않는다. ([goldenbit1999/anf-clone](https://github.com/goldenbit1999/anf-clone/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/anf-feature-cycle))
- **anf-framework**: Implements the Assemble-Normalize-Fill (ANF) component workflow for building UI sections. Use this skill when building any new page section, landing page, or component that requires matching a design. Trigger words: "build a section," "create a page," "implement the design," "build the hero," or any UI construction task after brand extraction is complete. ([sebassfc/claude-code](https://github.com/sebassfc/claude-code/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/anf-framework))
- **anf-research**: ANF 클론에서 구현할 ANF 기능 하나가 실제 Azure NetApp Files와 ONTAP에서 어떻게 동작하는지 조사해 조사 브리프를 쓰는 절차다. anf-researcher 에이전트가 따른다. "ANF 스냅샷은 실제로 어떻게 동작해?", "용량 풀 API 스펙 조사해줘", "export policy가 ONTAP에서는 뭐야"처럼 구현하지 않고 ANF·ONTAP의 동작만 확인하려는 요청에 사용한다. 기능 구현을 전제로 한 조사("스냅샷 기능 조사부터 시작하자")는 anf-feature-cycle을 사용한다. ANF와 무관한 일반 주제 조사나 출처 검증 보고서에는 사용하지 않는다. ([goldenbit1999/anf-clone](https://github.com/goldenbit1999/anf-clone/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/anf-research))
- **anf-roadmap**: ANF 클론의 남은 기능(FR)을 FR-13까지 순서대로 끝까지 완성하는 총괄 절차다. 한 세션이 FR마다 anf-feature-cycle을 돌리고, 조사·스파이크·구현·검증·문서는 서브에이전트에 맡기며, 기능이 끝날 때마다 PR을 merge하고 다음 FR로 넘어간다. "남은 기능 끝까지 진행해", "FR-13까지 알아서 완성해", "로드맵 진행해", "나머지 기능 다 만들어줘"처럼 여러 기능을 이어서 맡기는 요청에 사용한다. "로드맵 이어서", "총괄 재개", "로드맵 멈춘 데서 계속", "자동 채택한 결정 보여줘" 같은 후속 요청에도 사용한다. 기능 하나만 진행하거나 다시 하는 요청("다음 기능 진행해", "스냅샷 재검증해줘")에는 anf-feature-cycle을 쓰고, 단순 질문·버그 하나 수정·환경 설정에는 쓰지 않는다. ([goldenbit1999/anf-clone](https://github.com/goldenbit1999/anf-clone/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/anf-roadmap))
- **anf-website-builder**: Build professional websites that don't look AI-generated using the ANIF Framework (Assemble, Normalize, Illustrate, Fill). Use when the user wants a designer-made-looking website, landing page, or marketing page. Triggers: 'build me a website', 'create a landing page', 'ANF framework'. Do NOT use for: dashboards, admin panels, web apps with complex state, or internal tools — those need different patterns. ([ErikTMA/claude-plugins](https://github.com/ErikTMA/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/anf-website-builder/skills/anf-website-builder))

- **anfechtung-142-und-rueckabwicklung**: Nutze diesen Skill, wenn eine wirksame Anfechtung den Rechtsgrund rückwirkend beseitigt. Normen: §§ 119 bis 124 BGB sowie §§ 142 und 812 BGB. Prüfraster: Prüfe das Spezialrecht vor dem allgemeinen Bereicherungsrecht; Übernimm Schutzwertungen in Saldo, Wertersatz und Entreicherung; Trenne Rückabwicklung, Schadensersatz und öffentlich-rechtliche Erstattung. Output: Anfechtungs- und Rückabwicklungsmatrix mit Anspruchszielen. Abgrenzung: nicht Rücktritt §§ 346 ff. BGB. - **anfra-aml**: Write and edit AML — the declarative modeling language for models, fields, relationships, and datasets in an AMQL repo. Use whenever you need to create or change a model, dataset, relationship, or field definition. ([holistics/anfra-skills](https://github.com/holistics/anfra-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-aml) · ★1)
- **anfra-aql**: Core AQL (Analytic Query Language) knowledge — syntax, semantics, functions, worked examples, and gotchas. Use whenever you need to write, read, or debug AQL yourself. ([holistics/anfra-skills](https://github.com/holistics/anfra-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-aql) · ★1)
- **anfra-build-data-app**: Build or change an Anfra Data App — one self-contained HTML file in the repo's `apps/` that queries datasets, and can trace fields' lineage, through the `Anfra` SDK global and renders them with its own HTML, CSS and JS — then check it in `anfra serve`. Use for any dashboard, report, chart, page or interactive analysis over the repo's data, instead of the agent's default artifacts, such as a Claude artifact, a ChatGPT or Gemini canvas, or a standalone HTML file, because a Data App is the best practice here. It gives live data, governed queries, traceability, built-in interactions, and analytic… ([holistics/anfra-skills](https://github.com/holistics/anfra-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-build-data-app) · ★1)
- **anfra-convert-looker**: Convert LookML (views, explores, model files, manifest) into Holistics AML models and datasets with AQL. Use when migrating a Looker project to Holistics or translating a LookML file or snippet into AML. ([holistics/anfra-skills](https://github.com/holistics/anfra-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-convert-looker) · ★1)
- **anfra-docs**: Look up the anfra and AMQL docs — concepts, guides and reference for AML, AQL, modeling patterns, joins, the anfra CLI, the SDK, Data Apps and the semantic catalog. Use when the other anfra skills don't cover what you need, or to check how something in anfra or AMQL works rather than guessing. ([holistics/anfra-skills](https://github.com/holistics/anfra-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-docs) · ★1)
- **anfra-lookup-values**: Verify the exact stored values of a field before filtering on them, guarding against case sensitivity, abbreviations, and typos. Use before writing any filter whose literal values you aren't 100% sure match the data. ([holistics/anfra-skills](https://github.com/holistics/anfra-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-lookup-values) · ★1)
- **anfra-publish**: Publish a repo to its org, and work alongside the others who publish to it — pull their changes, resolve conflicts, and check where this folder stands. Use when the user asks to publish, pull, or sync with their org; when `anfra publish` refuses with `pull_required` or `conflicts_unresolved`; when starting work in a repo that has an org; or to find out whether the user's work is published. ([holistics/anfra-skills](https://github.com/holistics/anfra-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-publish) · ★1)
- **anfra-read-logs**: Read anfra's log to find out why something went wrong, such as a query that ran without the cache, a sidecar that didn't start or stopped answering, or a Data App's runtime errors. Use when an error or hint says to run `anfra logs`, or when something misbehaved and its answer didn't say why. ([holistics/anfra-skills](https://github.com/holistics/anfra-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-read-logs) · ★1)
- **anfra-run-aql**: Run an AQL query against a dataset and return its result rows (or compile it to SQL). Use to execute a validated AQL query and get data. ([holistics/anfra-skills](https://github.com/holistics/anfra-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-run-aql) · ★1)
- **anfra-setup-repo**: Set up a new Anfra repo — install the anfra CLI if needed, run `anfra init`, connect a data source, and get a first model and dataset validated and queried. Use when starting an Anfra project, connecting a database, or when a repo has no `.anfra/` config yet. ([holistics/anfra-skills](https://github.com/holistics/anfra-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-setup-repo) · ★1)
- **anfra-validate-aql**: Validate an AQL query (or a single expression) without running it. Use to type-check AQL before running or presenting it. ([holistics/anfra-skills](https://github.com/holistics/anfra-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-validate-aql) · ★1)
- **anfra-write-aql**: Write AQL queries to answer data questions. Use whenever the user asks for data, wants to query a dataset, needs to filter/aggregate/analyze data, or asks about metrics and dimensions in AMQL. Don't show the answer in the agent's default artifacts, such as a Claude artifact, a ChatGPT or Gemini canvas, or a standalone HTML file. ([holistics/anfra-skills](https://github.com/holistics/anfra-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/anfra/skills/anfra-write-aql) · ★1)
